Corporate Profile

華潤集團於中國的城市燃氣分銷旗艦                THE PRC CITY GAS DISTRIBUTION FLAGSHIP
                                OF CHINA RESOURCES GROUP
華潤燃氣控股有限公司（「華潤燃氣」）主要由中國最大       China Resources Gas Group Limited (“CR Gas”), substantially owned
的國有企業集團之一的華潤（集團）有限公司擁有。作為       by China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited, one of the largest
中國領先的燃氣公用事業集團，華潤燃氣主要經營業務        state-owned conglomerates in China, is a leading gas utilities group
包括天然氣購買及銷售、管道設施建設及運營、綜合服        in China, the principal businesses of which include the purchases
務、冷熱電綜合能源、車船用氣等。華潤燃氣的業務策        and sales of natural gas, the construction and operation of pipeline
略性地分佈於全國各地，主要位於經濟較發達和人口密        facilities, comprehensive services, combined cooling, heating and
集的地區以及天然氣儲量豐富的地區。               power, and gas for vehicles and vessels, etc. Its operations are
                                strategically located in areas of China, which are economically more
                                developed and densely populated and areas with rich reserves of
                                natural gas.

憑藉良好的行業基礎及本集團的執行能力，華潤燃氣於        Leveraging on the excellent industry fundamentals and the Group’s
二零二五年繼續通過內涵式增長實現穩步擴張。於二         execution competency, CR Gas continues to expand steadily in
零二五年末，華潤燃氣的城市燃氣項目總共達275 個，      2025 via organic growths. At the end of 2025, CR Gas’s portfolio
分佈於中國25 個省份，其中包括15 個省會城巿及76 個   consisted of 275 city gas projects in 25 provinces of China including
地級市，年燃氣總銷量達約401.8 億立方米及客戶數達     15 provincial capitals and 76 prefecture-level cities with an annual
6,271.9萬戶。                      gross gas sales volume of some 40.18 billion cubic meters and 62.719
                                million customers.

能源結構優化和環境污染治理將推動中國天然氣消費持        The optimization of energy structure and environmental pollution
續增長。中國政府近年來採取多項措施提倡對污染較少        control will promote the sustainable growth of natural gas consumption
的能源資源的發展及使用。同煤炭及原油等傳統能源         in China. The PRC government has, in recent years, taken various
資源相比，天然氣產業符合國家創新、協調、綠色、         measures to promote the development and utilization of less polluting
開放、共享的核心發展理念，國家在「十四·五」規劃和       energy sources. The natural gas industry is in sync with the core
2035 年遠景目標綱要草案中再次強調推動綠色低碳發      development idea of innovation, harmony, green, open and sharing
展，加強生態文明建設，天然氣的推廣及應用對保護生        as compared with conventional energy resources such as coal and
態環境、促進綠色轉型具有重要作用。               crude oil. In “The 14th Five-Year Plan” and the draft of “Long-Range
                                Objectives through the Year 2035”, the state once again emphasized
                                that the promotion of green and low-carbon development, the
                                enhancement of the construction of ecological civilization and the
                                promotion and application of natural gas played an important role in
                                protecting the ecological environment and promoting the transition to
                                green development.

中國仍是全球天然氣發展最重要區域，中國天然氣市場        The natural gas market in China is still in the important development
仍處於重要發展期，華潤燃氣將會充分利用有利經營環        period, as China remains the most important region in the global
境，透過內涵及外延式增長持續擴張、在追求卓越表現        rise of natural gas development. CR Gas is well placed to take
的同時努力提升其經營效率、提供安全可靠的清潔能源        advantage of such a favorable operating environment and to
供應並向客戶提供體貼、周到及高效的服務，向股東提        become the most respectable leader in the PRC’s gas industry
供不斷增長且可持續的業績表現，為僱員創造廣泛且專        by continuously expanding through organic and external growths;
業的個人發展機會，為中國及世界的環境保護及改善做        diligently enhancing its operational efficiency in pursue of
出貢獻，成為中國燃氣行業最受尊敬的領導者。           excellence; delivering a safe and reliable supply of clean energy as
                                well as the caring, competent and efficient service to our customers;
                                offering growing and sustainable performance to shareholders;
                                creating extensive professional and personal development
                                opportunities to employees; and contributing to the protection and
                                improvement of the Chinese and global environment.

    城市燃氣業務                                                    CITY GAS BUSINESS
    面對複雜多變的外部環境，本集團堅持穩                                        Amid a complex and ever-changing external environment, the Group
    健經營、主動應變，由前期預期寬鬆轉向                                        adhered to a principle of steady operation and proactive adaptation,
    供需緊平衡管理策略，強化氣源統籌、長                                        shifting from an earlier anticipatory loose management strategy
    協鎖定與應急保供機制，優化資源配置，                                        to a supply-demand tight balance approach. It strengthened gas
    嚴控運營成本，切實保障供氣安全與經營                                        source coordination, long-term agreement locking, and emergency
    穩定，持續提升抗風險能力與可持續發展                                        supply guarantee mechanisms, optimized resource allocation, strictly
    水平。                                                       controlled operating costs, effectively ensured gas supply safety and

董事會成員多元化政策                   BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY
本公司於二零一三年八月二十三日採納董           The Company adopted a board diversity policy (the “Policy”) on 23rd
事會成員多元化政策（「政策」）並於二零二         August, 2013 and updated the Policy on 26th December, 2022. A
二年十二月二十六日進行了更新修訂。此           summary of this Policy, together with the measurable objectives set for
政策之概要連同為執行此政策而制定之可           implementing this Policy, and the progress made towards achieving
計量目標及達標進度於下文披露。              those objectives are disclosed as below.


政策概要                         Summary of the Policy

本公司視董事會層面的多元化為維持競爭           The Company sees diversity at Board level as an essential element in
優勢的重要元素。一個真正多元化的董事           maintaining a competitive advantage. A truly diverse Board will include
會將包括具備不同技能、地區及行業經            and make good use of differences in the skills, regional and industry
驗、背景、性別及其他特質的董事會成            experience, background, gender and other qualities of the members
員，並可加以利用。該等差異將於釐定董           of the Board. These differences will be considered in determining
事會的最適合組成時予以考慮，並於可能           the optimum composition of the Board and when possible should be
情況下保持適當平衡。董事會所有委任均           balanced appropriately. All Board appointments are made on merit, in
按董事會整體有效運作所需要的技能及經           the content of the skills and experience the Board as a whole requires
驗水平作出。                       to be effective.


可計量目標                        Measurable Objectives

提名委員會將每年作出討論及協定為達致           The Nomination Committee will discuss and agree annually all
董事會成員多元化的所有可計量目標，並           measurable objectives for achieving diversity on the Board and
向董事會建議有關目標以供採納。於任何           recommend them to the Board for adoption. At any given time, the
特定時間，董事會可尋求改善其於一方面           Board may seek to improve one or more aspects of its diversity
或多方面的多元化，並相應計量進度。於           and measure progress accordingly. For the year under review, no
回顧年度，提名委員會並無制定可計量目           measurable objective was set by the Nomination Committee.
標。


於二零二五年底，本公司高級管理人員共           As at the end of 2025, the Company has 417 senior management in
有417 名，包括50 名女性及367 名男性（男    total comprising of 50 females and 367 males (a male-to-female ratio
女比例為7.3：1）。                  of 7.3 : 1).


於二零二五年底，本公司共有55,928 名僱       As at the end of 2025, the Company has 55,928 employees in total
員，包括約18,610 名女性及37,318 名男性   comprising of approximately 18,610 females and 37,318 males (a
（男女比例為 2：1），反映出本公司普遍堅        male-to-female ratio of 2 : 1), reflecting a gender equality principle
持的性別平等原則，以及本集團所經營的           generally adhered to by the Company and the relatively low female
燃氣行業，女性從業人員比例整體相對較           gender workforce ratio in the gas industry which the Group operates.
低。本公司在評估僱員的候選資格時注意           The Company is mindful of the objectives as set out in the diversity
到多元化政策所載目標，並將確保本公司           policy when assessing the candidacy of the employees, and will
繼續遵循該政策。                     ensure that the Company shall continue to follow the policy.


董事會將每年檢討董事會多元化政策的實           The Board will annually review the implementation and effectiveness
施及有效性。董事會認為有關政策有效達           of the Board diversity policy. The Board considers that the policy is
至董事會多元化。                     effective to achieve diversity at the Board.

     獲得獨立的觀點及意見。經董事會主席批准                        views and input are available to the Board. Subject to approval of
     後，在適當情況下，董事如有需要可尋求獨                        the Chairman of the Board, Directors may seek, at the Company’s
     立於本公司顧問的獨立法律、財務或其他專                        expense, independent legal, financial or other professional advices
     業意見，費用由本公司承擔，以促使其有效                        from advisors independent from those advising the Company as and
     履行其職責。董事會將每年檢討該機制的實                        when necessary in appropriate circumstances to enable them to
     施及有效性。                                     discharge their responsibilities effectively. The Board will review the
                                                implementation and effectiveness of such mechanisms on an annual
                                                basis.

     提名政策                                       Nomination Policy

     提名政策旨在確保董事會在技能、經驗、                         Nomination policy aims to ensure that the Board has a balance
     知識及多元化觀點方面取得平衡，切合本                         of skills, experience, knowledge and diversity of perspectives
     公司的業務要求。                                   appropriate to the requirements of the Company’s business.


     提名委員會將會妥為考慮以下條件（統稱為                        The Nomination Committee will evaluate, select and recommend
     「該等條件」）以評核、甄選及向董事會建議                       candidate(s) for directorships to the Board by giving due consideration
     一名或多名候選人擔任董事，該等條件包                         to criteria including but not limited to (collectively, the “Criteria”):
     括但不限於：


     (a)   多元化觀點，包括但不限於性別、年                     (a)   Diversity in aspects including but not limited to gender, age,
           齡、文化背景及教育背景、專業經                            cultural and educational background, professional experience,
           驗、技能、知識及服務年期；                              skills, knowledge and length of service;


     (b)   就可用時間及有關利益而言，對於董                     (b)   Commitment for responsibilities of the Board in respect of
           事會的職責的承擔；                                  available time and relevant interest;


     (c)   於本公司業務所涉及的行業之成就及                     (c)   Accomplishment and experience in the relevant industries in
           經驗以及其他專業資格；                                which the Company’s business is involved and other professional
                                                      qualifications;


     (d)   誠信方面的聲譽；                             (d)   Reputation for integrity;


     (e)   該（等）人士可以為董事會帶來的潛在                    (e)   Potential contributions that the individual(s) can bring to the
           貢獻；                                        Board;


     (f)   對於董事會繼任有序予以落實的一項                     (f)   Plan(s) in place for the orderly succession of the Board.
           或多項計劃。


     上述因素只供參考，並不旨在涵蓋所有因                         The above Criteria are for reference only, and not meant to be
     素，也不具決定性作用。提名委員會可決                         exhaustive and decisive. The Nomination Committee has the discretion
     定提名任何其認為適當的人士。                             to nominate any person, as it considers appropriate.


     提名委員會將會妥為考慮以下條件以評核                         The Nomination Committee will evaluate and recommend retiring
     及向董事會建議一名或多名退任的董事接                         Director(s) to the Board for re-appointment by giving due
     受再度委任，條件包括但不限於：                            consideration to the criteria including but not limited to:


     (a)   該（等）退任的董事對於本公司的整體                    (a)   The overall contribution and service of the retiring Director(s)
           貢獻及服務，包括但不限於出席董事                           to the Company, including but not limited to the attendance
           會的會議及 ╱ 或其屬下委員會的會議                         of the meetings of the Board and/or its committees and
           及股東大會（如適用），以及在董事會                          general meetings where applicable, in addition to the level
           及 ╱ 或其屬下委員會的參與程度及表                         of participation and performance on the Board and/or its
           現；                                         committees;


     (b)   該（等）退任的董事是否繼續符合該等                    (b)   Whether the retiring Director(s) continue(s) to satisfy the Criteria.
           條件。

     環境、社會及管治委員會                                   Environmental, Social and Governance Committee

      於二零二五年三月二十八日，企業管治                            On 28th March, 2025, the Corporate Governance Committee was
      委員會更名為環境、社會及管治委員會                            renamed as the Environmental, Social and Governance Committee (the

      職能及責任。ESG 委員會的主要職能為協                         adjusted. The role and main function of the ESG Committee is to assist
      助董事會指導及制定本公司的環境、社會                           the Board in providing guidance on and formulating the vision, goals,
      及管治願景、目標、策略及框架，以及確                           strategies and framework of the Company in relation to environmental,
      保其符合公司的需要及遵守適用法律、法                           social and governance, and ensuring that they are in line with the
      規、監管規定及國際標準；監督本公司環                           needs of the Company and comply with applicable laws, regulations,
      境、社會及管治願景、策略及框架的制定                           regulatory requirements, and international standards; monitoring the
      與實施；審議、批准並授權發佈華潤燃氣                           development and implementation of the Company’s environmental,
     《環境、社會及管治報告》、重要性議題的                           social and governance vision, strategies and framework; considering,
      優先次序及相關信息披露，並向董事會提                           approving and authorizing the publication of the Environmental, Social
      出相關建議。                                       and Governance Report of CR Gas, the prioritization of material issues

風險管理及內部監控                                  RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
董事會整體負責維持有效的風險管理及內                         The Board has the overall responsibility of maintaining an effective risk
部監控系統，為重大的失實陳述或損失作                         management and internal control system to provide reasonable, though
出合理而非絕對的保證，並管理而非消除                         not absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss and to
未能達到業務目標的風險。上述風險包括                         manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business
但不限於與ESG有關的重大風險。                           objectives. The above risks include, but are not limited to, material risks
                                           related to ESG.


董事會透過審核與風險管理委員會，對本                         The Board, through the Audit and Risk Management Committee,
公司風險管理及內部監控系統對應的所有                         conducts annual review of the effectiveness of the Company’s systems
重大監控的有效性進行年度檢討，包括本                         of risk management and internal control covering all material controls,
公司的財務監控、內部監控及風險管理系                         including financial controls, internal control and risk management
統。                                         systems.


審核與風險管理委員會應董事會的委派或                         The Audit and Risk Management Committee considers the major
主動就有關風險管理及內部監控事宜的重                         investigation findings on risk management and internal control matter
要調查結果及管理層對調查結果的回應進                         as delegated by the Board or on its own initiative and management’s
行研究。                                       response to these findings.


審核與風險管理委員會亦考慮本公司會                          The Audit and Risk Management Committee also considers the
計、財務申報和內部審核職能以及與本公                         adequacy of resources, qualifications and experience of the
司 ESG 表現和匯報相關的資源、資格和經                      Company’s accounting, financial reporting and internal audit functions
驗及彼等的培訓計劃和預算是否充足。就                         as well as those relating to the Company's ESG performance and
處理及發佈內幕消息的程序及內部監控而                         reporting, and their training programmes and budget. With respect

     股東召開股東特別大會的程序                              Procedures for shareholders to convene special general meeting

     根據百慕達一九八一年公司法（「公司法」）                       In accordance with the Section 74 of the Companies Act 1981 of
     第 74 條，於遞呈要求日期持有本公司不少                      Bermuda (“Companies Act”), the shareholders of the Company holding
     於（賦於本公司股東大會上之投票權）十分                        at the date of the deposit of the requisition not less than one-tenth of
     之一繳足股本的本公司股東有權召開本公司                        such of the paid-up capital of the Company carrying the right of voting
     股東特別大會。倘董事於上述要求之送達日                        at general meetings of the Company, shall have the right to convene
     期二十一日內未有召開會議，代表總投票權                        a special general meeting of the Company. If the directors do not
     半數以上的呈請人可自行召開會議。                           within twenty-one days from the date of the deposit of the requisition
                                                proceed duly to convene a meeting, the requisitionists representing
                                                more than one half of the total voting rights may themselves convene
                                                a meeting.

     股息政策                                       DIVIDEND POLICY

長期獎勵計劃                            LONG TERM AWARD SCHEME
於二零零八年十二月一日，本公司採納一                On 1st December, 2008, the Company adopted a share repurchased
項股份購回現金獎勵計劃（「獎勵計劃」），              cash award scheme (the “Incentive Award Scheme”), pursuant to
據此，本集團或投資公司的經甄選僱員皆                which selected employees of the Group or investee companies (being
有權根據計劃規則參加獎勵計劃。投資公                any company in which the Company directly or indirectly owns 5%
司乃指任何一家由本公司直接或間接擁有                or more attributable equity interests (the “Investee Company”), and
5%或以上歸屬權益的公司（「投資公司」），             in calculating the percentage interest so attributed, the percentage
就計算歸屬權益百分比而言，在投資公司                interest held in the Investee Company shall be multiplied by the
所持有權益的百分比須乘以本集團透過其                percentage interest in each level of entity through which such interest
他公司持有該權益的該等中間公司的百分                is held by the Group), are entitled to participate in the Incentive Award
比。                                Scheme pursuant to the scheme rules.

獎勵計劃旨在嘉許若干僱員的貢獻及鼓勵                The purpose of the Incentive Award Scheme is to recognise and
彼等作出貢獻，並提供獎勵及協助本集團                motivate the contribution of certain employees and to provide
及投資公司挽留其現有僱員及延聘更多僱                incentives and to enable the Group and the Investee Company in
員，並就達到本公司的長期商業目標向彼                retaining their existing employees and recruiting additional employees
等提供直接經濟利益。除非被董事會提前                and to provide them with a direct economic interest in attaining
終止，獎勵計劃自採納日期起十年內有                 the long-term business objectives of the Company. Unless early
效。根據獎勵計劃授出獎勵股份及於其整                termination by the Board, the Incentive Award Scheme shall be valid
個有效期內授予經甄選僱員獎勵股份的總                and effective for a term of 10 years commencing on the adoption
數上限分別以本公司不時已發行股本的5%               date. The maximum aggregate numbers of award shares which can
及0.5%為限。                          be awarded under the Incentive Award Scheme and to a selected
                                  employee throughout its duration are limited to 5% and 0.5% of the
                                  issued share capital of the Company from time to time respectively.

     核數師就審計綜合財務報表須承擔的責任                         Auditor’s responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated
                                                financial statements

     我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否                         Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the
     不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤                         consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material
     陳述取得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見                         misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s
     的核數師報告。我們是按照百慕達1981 年                      report that includes our opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as
     《公司法案》第90 條的規定，僅向整體股東                      a body, in accordance with Section 90 of the Bermuda Companies
     報告。除此以外，我們的報告不可用作其                         Act 1981, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility
     他用途。我們概不就本報告的內容，對任                         towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this
     何其他人士負責或承擔法律責任。                            report.


     合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按                         Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a
     照《香港審計準則》進行的審計，在某一重                        guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will
     大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可                         always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements
     以由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們                         can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if,
     單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使                         individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to
     用者依賴財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則                         influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these
     有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                             consolidated financial statements.


     在 根 據《 香 港 審 計 準 則 》進 行 審 計 的 過 程          As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise
     中，我們運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷                         professional judgement and maintain professional skepticism
     疑態度。我們亦：                                   throughout the audit. We also:


     •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜                       •    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the
         合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風                             consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,
         險，設計及執行審計程序以應對這些                            design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks,
         風險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑                            and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate
         證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐                            to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a
         可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛                            material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for
         假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因                            one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery,
         此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯誤                            intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of
         陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導                            internal control.
         致的重大錯誤陳述的風險。


     •   了解與審計相關的內部控制，以設計                       •    Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit

      1. 一般資料                                    1. GENERAL
          本公司為一家於百慕達註冊成立的獲                            The Company is a listed public company incorporated in
          豁免的上市有限公司，其股份於香港                            Bermuda as an exempted company with limited liability and its
          聯 合 交 易 所 有 限 公 司（「 聯 交 所 」）上               shares are listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited
          市，其中間母公司為於香港註冊成立                            (the “Stock Exchange”). The Company’s intermediate parent
          的華潤（集團）有限公司（「華潤集團公                          company is China Resources (Holdings) Company Limited
          司」）；而其最終控股公司為中國華潤                           (“CRH”), a company incorporated in Hong Kong, and its ultimate
          有限公司（「中國華潤」），該公司為一                          holding company is China Resources Company Limited (“CRCL”),
          家於中國成立的公司。本公司註冊辦                            a company established in the PRC. The addresses of the
          事處及主要營業地點的地址於本年報                            registered office and principal place of business of the Company
          內公司資料一節披露。                                  are disclosed in the Corporate Information of the annual report.


          本公司為一家投資控股公司，其主要                            The Company is an investment holding company. The activities
          附屬公司、合營公司及聯營公司的業                            of its principal subsidiaries, joint ventures and associates are set
          務分別載於附註47、48及49。                            out in notes 47, 48 and 49, respectively.


          綜合財務報表以港元（「港元」）呈列，                          The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong
          原因為本公司為一家股份於香港聯交                            Kong dollars (“HK$”) as the Company is a public company with
          所上市的上市公司，而其大部分投資                            its shares listed on the Stock Exchange in Hong Kong where
          者亦在香港，因此本公司董事認為在                            most of its investors are located, and therefore, the directors of
          呈列本公司及其附屬公司（統稱「本集                           the Company consider that HK$ is preferable in presenting the
          團」）經營業績及財務狀況時使用港元                           operating results and financial position of the Company and its
          更為合適。                                       subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”).


      2. 編製基準                                    2. BASIS OF PREPARATION
          於編製本綜合財務報表時，有見及於二                           In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors
          零二五年十二月三十一日本集團的流                            of the Company have given careful consideration to the future
          動負債超逾其流動資產24,928,893,000                    liquidity of the Group in light of the fact that the Group’s current
          港元及本集團有資本承擔45,394,000                       liabilities exceeded its current assets by HK$24,928,893,000,
          港元，本公司董事已審慎考慮本集團                            and the Group had capital commitments of HK$45,394,000 as
          的未來流動資金狀況。於二零二五年                            at 31st December, 2025. As at 31st December, 2025, the Group
          十二月三十一日，本集團的銀行及其                            had bank and other borrowings of HK$23,700,953,000, of which
          他借貸為23,700,953,000港元，其中                     HK$15,476,408,000 was classified as current liabilities.
          15,476,408,000港元被分類為流動負

          本公司董事認為，經計及本集團未動                            The directors of the Company are of the opinion that, taking into

          除於各個報告期末按公平值計算的若                            The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on
          干金融工具外，綜合財務報表乃根據                            the historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments

          歷史成本一般以交換貨品及服務時給                            Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the
          予代價的公平值為基準。                                 consideration given in exchange for goods and services.


          公平值是於計量日期市場參與者於有                            Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset
          秩序交易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負                            or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
          債須支付的價格，而不論該價格是否                            market participants at the measurement date, regardless of
          直接可觀察或可使用其他估值技術估                            whether that price is directly observable or estimated using
          計。若市場參與者於計量日期對資產                            another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value
          或負債定價時會考慮資產或負債的特                            of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the
          點，則本集團於估計資產或負債的公                            characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants
          平值時會考慮該等特點。此等綜合財                            would take those characteristics into account when pricing
          務報表中作計量及 ╱ 或披露用途的公                          the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for
          平值乃按此基準釐定，惟屬於香港財                            measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated
          務報告準則第2號「以股份為基礎的付                           financial statements is determined on such a basis, except
          款」
           （「香港財務報告準則第2號」）範圍                          for share-based payment transactions that are within the
          的以股份付款的交易、屬於香港財務                            scope of HKFRS 2 Share-based payment (“HKFRS 2”), leasing
          報告準則第 16 號「租賃」範圍內的租                         transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS 16 Leases, and
          賃交易，以及與公平值有部分相若地                            measurements that have some similarities to fair value but are
          方但並非公平值的計量，譬如香港會                            not fair value, such as net realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories
          計準則第2 號「存貨」內的可變現淨額                          or value in use in HKAS 36 Impairment of assets (“HKAS 36”).
          或香港會計準則第 36 號「資產減值」
          的使用價值除外（「香港會計準則第36

  合併基準                     Basis of consolidation

  綜合財務報表包括本公司及其所控制         The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
  實體及其附屬公司的財務報表。倘本         statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
  公司符合以下條件時，即取得控制          Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved where the
  權：                       Company:


  －    可對投資對象行使權力；         –    has power over the investee;


  －    因參與投資對象業務而承受浮動      –    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
       回報的風險或享有權利；及             involvement with the investee; and


  －    有能力動用其權力影響其回報。      –    has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.


  倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制因         The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
  素之一項或多項出現變化，本集團將         facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one
  重新評估其是否控制投資對象。           or more of the three elements of control listed above.

          倘本集團於投資對象的投票權未能佔                            When the Group has less than a majority of the voting rights
          大多數，但只要投票權足以賦予本集                            of an investee, it has power over the investee when the voting
          團實際能力可單方面掌控投資對象之                            rights are sufficient to give it the practical ability to direct the
          相關業務時，本集團即對投資對象擁                            relevant activities of the investee unilaterally. Generally, there is
          有權力。一般而言，推定多數投票權                            a presumption that the majority of voting rights results in control.
          形成控制權。在評估本集團於投資對                            The Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances in
          象的投票權是否足以賦予其權力時，                            assessing whether or not the Group’s voting rights in an investee

          括：

          業務合併                                         Business combinations

          並非合併共同控制業務的收購業務採                             Acquisitions of businesses that are not combinations of business
          用收購法入賬。業務合併中之轉讓代                             under common control are accounted for using the acquisition
          價按公平值計量，而計算方法為本集                             method. The consideration transferred in a business combination
          團所轉讓資產、本集團自被收購方原                             is measured at fair value, which is calculated as the sum of
          股東承接之負債及本集團為交換被收                             the acquisition-date fair values of the assets transferred by the

  取合約現金流量及出售。並非按上述        a business model with the objective of both holding to collect

  平值計入損益計量。               held within the aforementioned business models are classified

  金融資產減值                  Impairment of financial assets

  本集團確認對並非按公平值計入損益        The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses
  持有的所有債務工具預期信貸虧損         (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
 （「預期信貸虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸       profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
  虧損乃基於根據合同到期的合同現金        contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and
  流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流        all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted
  量之間的差額而釐定，並以原實際利        at an approximation of the original effective interest rate. The
  率的近似值貼現。預期現金流量將包        expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of
  括出售所持抵押的現金流量或組成合        collateral held or other credit enhancements that are integral to
  同條款的其他信貸提升措施。           the contractual terms.


 一般方法                     General approach

  預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自        ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for
  初步確認起未有顯著增加的信貸風險        which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk
  而言，就未來12 個月內可能發生的       since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses
  違約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12 個月      that result from default events that are possible within the next
  預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。        12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for
  就自初步確認起已經顯著增加的信貸        which there has been a significant increase in credit risk since
  風險而言，不論何時發生違約，於餘        initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses
  下風險年期內的預期信貸虧損均須計        expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of
  提虧損撥備（整個存續期預期信貸虧        the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).
  損）。


  於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具        At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit
  的信貸風險自初步確認起是否出現顯        risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly since
  著增加。作此評估時，本集團比較金        initial recognition. When making the assessment, the Group
  融工具於報告日期出現違約的風險與        compares the risk of a default occurring on the financial
  該金融工具於初步確認日期出現違約        instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a default
  的風險，並考慮毋須花費不必要成本        occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of initial
  或精力即可獲得的合理及有理據的資        recognition and considers reasonable and supportable
  料，包括過往及前瞻性資料。本集團        information that is available without undue cost or effort,
  認為倘合同付款逾期超過30 天時信貸      including historical and forward-looking information. The Group
  風險已顯著增加。                considers that there has been a significant increase in credit risk
                          when contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.

  當負債項下的責任已解除、取消或屆      A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the
  滿，即會終止確認金融負債。         liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.


  倘一項現有金融負債被來自同一貸方      When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from
  且大部分條款不同之另一項金融負債      the same lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of
  所取代，或現有負債之條款被大幅修      an existing liability are substantially modified, such an exchange
  改，則該項置換或修改視作終止確認      or modification is treated as a derecognition of the original
  原有負債及確認新增負債處理，而相      liability and a recognition of a new liability, and the difference

  確認的款項，且有意清償該款項的淨      amount is reported in the statement of financial position if there

  債，並於財務狀況表內呈列淨額。       realise the assets and settle the liabilities simultaneously.


  借貸成本                  Borrowing costs

  或出售前須較長準備時間的合資格資      construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets
  產直接產生的借貸成本乃計入有關資      that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready for
  產的成本，直至有關資產已大致可作      their intended use or sale, are added to the costs of those assets
  其擬定用途或出售為止。           until such time as the assets are substantially ready for their
                        intended use or sale.

  前進行的短期投資所賺取的投資收       specific borrowings pending their expenditure on qualifying
  入，將從撥充資本的借貸成本扣除。      assets is deducted from the borrowing costs eligible for

  中確認。                  period in which they are incurred.

          根據中華人民共和國企業所得稅法                              Under the Law of the People’s Republic of China on Enterprise
         （「 企 業 所 得 稅 法 」）及 企 業 所 得 稅 法                Income Tax (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the
          實施條例，中國附屬公司的稅率為                              EIT Law, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries is 25% except for
          25%，惟若干集團實體享有各種優惠                            certain group entities which are entitled to various concessionary
          稅率或稅項減免。                                     tax rates or tax exemptions and reliefs.


          根據財政部及國家稅務總局聯合下                              According to a joint circular of the Ministry of Finance and the
          發的通知（財稅二零一一年第 1 號），                          State Administration of Taxation, Cai Shui 2011 No. 1, only the
          外資企業僅於向外國投資者分派其於                             profits earned by foreign-investment enterprises prior to 1st
          二零零八年一月一日前賺取的溢利時                             January, 2008, when distributed to foreign investors, can be
          方可不溯既往並獲豁免繳納預扣稅；                             grandfathered and exempted from withholding tax. Whereas
          而於該日後根據所產生溢利而分派的                             dividend distributed out of the profits generated thereafter,
          股息則須根據新稅法第 3 條及第 27 條                        shall be subject to the Enterprise Income Tax at 5% or 10% and
          以及其詳細實施規則第 91 條按 5% 或                        withheld by the PRC entities, pursuant to Articles 3 and 27 of the
          10% 的稅率繳納企業所得稅（由中國                           New Law and Article 91 of its Detailed Implementation Rules.
          實體扣除）。

          約。通常已提前作出一次性付款以                                  sum payments were generally made upfront to acquire the leased

          年，而根據該等土地租賃的條款，將                                 ongoing payments will be made under the terms of these land

          通常為 2 至 20 年，而廠房、機器及設                            2 and 20 years, and leases of plant, machinery and equipment

20. 商譽（續）                          20. GOODWILL (CONTINUED)
  現金產生單位的可收回金額乃按使用                    The recoverable amounts of the CGUs are determined based on
  價值計算方法釐定。本集團聘請獨立                    value-in-use calculations. The Group engaged an independent
  專業估值師協助計算若干現金產生單                    professional valuer to assist with the calculation for certain CGUs.
  位。計算使用價值時的主要假設與未                    The key assumptions for the value-in-use calculations are those
  來收入增長率及折現率有關。管理層                    regarding the future revenue growth rates and discount rates.
  乃採用可反映有關貨幣時值及現金產                    Management estimates discount rates using pre-tax rates that
  生單位特有的風險的現行市場評估的                    reflect current market assessments of the time value of money
  稅前利率估計折現率。未來收入增長                    and the risks specific to the CGUs. The future revenue growth
  率按業內增長預測以及管理層過往慣                    rates are based on industry growth forecasts and management’s
  例及預期市場未來變化而釐定。                      past practices and expectations of future changes in the market.


  本集團根據高級管理層批准有關未來                    The Group prepares cash flow forecasts derived from the most
  五年（銷售液化氣及燃氣接駁業務的                    recent financial budgets approved by senior management for
  一般發展期）的最近期財政預算編製                    the next 5 years, which is the general development period for
  現金流量預測，及按首五年的估計未                    the sale of liquefied gas and the gas connection business, and
  來收入增長率0%-30%及按餘下年度                  extrapolates cash flows for the period beyond the budget period
  的 估 計 未 來 收 入 增 長 率 2 %（ 二 零 二      based on the estimated future revenue growth rates of 0%-
  四年：首五年為0%-20%及剩餘年度                  30% for the first 5 years and 2% for the remaining years (2024:
  為2%）來推斷預算期後期間的現金流                   0%-20% for the first 5 years and 2% for the remaining years).
  量。預測現金產生單位的現金流量所                    The pre-tax rate used to discount the forecast cash flows for
  用的稅前折現率為 7.9%-9.5%（二零               the CGUs is 7.9%-9.5% (2024: 8.1%-10.1%). In the opinion of
  二四年：8.1%-10.1%）。董事認為，               the directors, no material impairment loss on goodwill on gas
  於報告期末，燃氣業務的商譽並未識                    operation was identified at the end of the reporting period.
  別任何重大減值虧損。

      37. 長期獎勵計劃                                 37. LONG-TERM AWARD SCHEME
          於二零零八年十二月一日，本公司採                            On 1st December, 2008, the Company adopted the share
          納 股 份 購 回 現 金 獎 勵 計 劃（「 獎 勵 計               repurchased cash award scheme (“Incentive Award Scheme”)
          劃」），作為挽留並鼓勵員工的獎勵，                           as an incentive to retain and encourage the employees for the
          以實現本集團持續運營及發展。                              continual operation and development of the Group.


          根據獎勵計劃，本公司現有股份將由                            Pursuant to the Incentive Award Scheme, existing shares of
          受託人利用本集團所注入的現金從市                            the Company will be purchased by the trustee from the market
          場購買，並以信託方式為特定僱員持                            out of cash contributed by the Group and be held in trust for
          有，直至股份歸屬於特定僱員為止。                            the selected employees until such shares are vested with the
          倘計劃有效期內所授出限制性股份總                            selected employees. No restricted shares will be granted if the
          數超過本公司於計劃採納日期已發行                            aggregate number of restricted shares awarded throughout
          股本之 5%，則不得再授出限制性股                           the duration of the scheme is to be in excess of 5% of the
          份。根據獎勵計劃可授予特定僱員的                            issued share capital of the Company at the adoption date of the
          限制性股份的最大數目不得超過本公                            scheme. The maximum number of restricted shares which may
          司不時已發行股本之0.5%。                              be awarded to a selected employee under the Incentive Award
                                                      Scheme shall not exceed 0.5% of the issued share capital of the
                                                      Company from time to time.


          於二零一零年三月十六日，本公司對                            On 16th March, 2010, the Company amended the Incentive
          獎勵計劃作出修訂，將用現金取代受                            Award Scheme such that cash instead of restricted shares will be
          限制股份作為獎勵。本公司於根據獎                            awarded. The Company will utilise the proceeds generated from
          勵計劃作出現金獎勵時，將動用出售                            disposal of the restricted shares held by the trustee for making
          由受託人持有的限制性股份產生的所                            cash awards under the Incentive Award Scheme.
          得款項。

      43. 資本風險管理                                 43. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT
          本集團管理其資本以維持經營業務所                            The Group manages its capital to maintain a balance between
          得現金流量的持續性及使用銀行及其                            continuity of cash flows from operating activities and the flexibility
          他借貸靈活性之間的平衡。本集團亦                            through the use of bank and other borrowings. The Group also
          定期監控現時及預期流動資金需求，                            monitors the current and expected liquidity requirements and
          並檢查對貸款契約的遵守情況，以確                            its compliance with lending covenants regularly to ensure that
          保具備足夠的營運資金及充足的承諾                            sufficient working capital and adequate committed lines of
          信貸額度以滿足其流動資金需求。本                            funding are maintained to meet its liquidity requirements. The
          集團的總體策略與往年保持一致。                             Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior years.


          本集團的資本架構包括負債（包括銀                            The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which
          行及其他借貸）及中期票據，不包括                            include bank and other borrowings, medium-term notes, net of
          銀行結餘及本公司擁有人應佔權益                             bank balances and equity attributable to owners of the Company,
         （由已發行資本、保留溢利及其他儲備                            comprising issued capital, retained profits and other reserves.
          組成）。


          本集團管理層定期檢討資本架構。管                            The management of the Group reviews the capital structure on a
          理層認為資本成本及相關風險乃檢討                            regular basis. As part of this review, management considers the
          內容的一部分，並採取適當行動調整                            cost of capital and the associated risks, and takes appropriate
          本集團的資本架構，包括派付股息、                            actions to adjust the Group’s capital structure through the
          發行新股、發行新債或償清現有債務。                           payment of dividends, new share issues as well as the issue of
                                                      new debts or the redemption of existing debts.

               現重大增加，則其信貸質素被                                    significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition.
               視為「正常」。

        本集團以經常性基準按公平值計                  Fair value of the Group’s financial assets that are measured
        量的金融資產的公平值（續）                   at fair value on a recurring basis (Continued)

        公平值計量根據公平值計量的輸                  Fair value measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2, or
        入數據可觀察程度及公平值計量                  3 based on the degree to which the inputs to the fair value
        的輸入數據對其整體的重要性分                  measurements are observable and the significance of the
        類為第一級、第二級或第三級，                  inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety, which
        詳情如下：                           are described as follows:


        (i)     第一級輸入數據是實體於             (i)     Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active
                計量日期可以取得的相同                     markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity
                資產或負債於活躍市場之                     can access at the measurement date;
                報價（未經調整）；


        (ii)    第二級輸入數據是就資產             (ii)    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices
                或負債直接或間接地可觀                     included within Level 1, that are observable for the
                察之輸入數據（第一級內包                    asset or liability, either directly or indirectly;
                括的報價除外）；


        (iii)   第三級輸入數據是資產或             (iii)   Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or
                負債的不可觀察輸入數據。                    liability.


        於本年度及過往年度，第一級、                  There were no transfers among Levels 1, 2 and 3 in the
        第二級及第三級之間並無任何轉                  current and prior years.
        撥。

        產及金融負債（附註33 所披露的                (except for medium-term notes as disclosed in note 33)
        中期票據除外）的賬面值與彼等                  recorded at amortised cost in the consolidated financial
        的公平值相若。                         statements approximate to their fair values.
